```python
"""
Search for non-negative step-function heights minimizing
    2*n*max(conv(a,a)) / (sum(a))^2.

Main ideas:
- Fast FFT-based objective and subconvex loops.
- Diverse coarse starts: beta/ramp shapes, two-blocks, sinusoids, blocks,
  low-dimensional differential-evolution starts, and upsampling of known
  best sequences.
- Local search by multiplicative projected-subgradient steps with adaptive
  active-coefficient weights.
- Periodic Frank-Wolfe-like directions from the LP
      max 1^T h   s.t.  a*h <= 1, h >= 0,
  followed by exact line search from current sequence to the LP direction.
- For large lengths, coarse block LP directions are used.
- Final candidate is validated with the exact evaluator and sanitized.
"""

import numpy as np
import time

try:
    from scipy.optimize import linprog
except Exception:
    linprog = None

try:
    from scipy.optimize import differential_evolution
except Exception:
    differential_evolution = None


def _pair_score(pair):
    return pair[0]


def normalize_to_sum(a, target=1.0):
    """Clip to finite nonnegative values and rescale to target sum."""
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    if a.ndim != 1:
        a = a.ravel()
    if a.size == 0:
        return np.ones(1, dtype=np.float64) * float(target)

    a = np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
    a = np.clip(a, 0.0, 1000.0)

    s = float(a.sum())
    if s <= 1e-300:
        return np.full(a.size, float(target) / float(a.size), dtype=np.float64)

    a = a * (float(target) / s)
    a = np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
    return a


def fast_conv_self(a):
    """FFT linear convolution a * a, truncated to length 2n-1."""
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    n = a.size
    if n == 0:
        return np.empty(0, dtype=np.float64)
    L = 2 * n - 1
    N = 1 << (L - 1).bit_length()
    fa = np.fft.rfft(a, N)
    fa = fa * fa
    c = np.fft.irfft(fa, N)[:L]
    c = np.maximum(c, 0.0)
    return np.ascontiguousarray(c)


def fft_convolve(x, y):
    """FFT linear convolution of two real arrays."""
    x = np.asarray(x, dtype=np.float64)
    y = np.asarray(y, dtype=np.float64)
    if x.size == 0 or y.size == 0:
        return np.empty(0, dtype=np.float64)

    L = x.size + y.size - 1
    N = 1 << (L - 1).bit_length()
    fx = np.fft.rfft(x, N)
    fy = np.fft.rfft(y, N)
    out = np.fft.irfft(fx * fy, N)[:L]
    out = np.maximum(out, 0.0)
    return np.ascontiguousarray(out)


def score_and_conv(a, n):
    """Return (score, convolution) using the evaluator formula."""
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    n = int(a.size)

    s = float(a.sum())
    if s <= 1e-300:
        return float("inf"), np.zeros(2 * n - 1, dtype=np.float64)

    b = fast_conv_self(a)
    if b.size == 0:
        return float("inf"), b

    maxb = float(np.max(b))
    sc = float(2.0 * n * maxb / (s * s))
    if not np.isfinite(sc):
        sc = float("inf")
    return sc, b


def compute_r(a, lam):
    """
    r_i = sum_j a_j * lam_{i+j}, where lam has length 2n-1.
    This is the derivative factor for a weighted sum of convolution coefficients.
    """
    n = len(a)
    if n == 0:
        return np.empty(0, dtype=np.float64)
    if n == 1:
        return np.array([float(a[0] * lam[0])], dtype=np.float64)

    rev = lam[::-1]
    conv = fft_convolve(a, rev)
    start = 2 * n - 2
    if conv.size <= start:
        return np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)

    seg = conv[start : n - 2 : -1]
    if seg.size != n:
        seg = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    return np.ascontiguousarray(secg.copy()) if False else np.ascontiguousarray(seg)


def eval_mix_scores(A, B, C, T, n):
    """
    Evaluate max coefficient of max_k (A t^2 + B t + C) for t in T.
    A, B, C represent the quadratic expansion of conv((1-t)a + t d).
    """
    Tn = np.asarray(T, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1, 1)
    vals = C.reshape(1, -1) + B.reshape(1, -1) * Tn + A.reshape(1, -1) * (Tn * Tn)
    m = np.max(vals, axis=1)
    m = np.where(np.isfinite(m), m, np.inf)
    return (2.0 * float(n)) * m


def line_search(a, b_a, d, b_d, n, cr=None):
    """
    Line search over t in [0,1] for (1-t)a + t d.
    a and d are assumed to have sum 1.
    Returns (best_t, best_seq, best_conv, best_score).
    """
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    d = np.asarray(d, dtype=np.float64)

    if cr is None:
        cr = fft_convolve(a, d)

    A = b_d - 2.0 * cr + b_a
    B = 2.0 * (cr - b_a)
    C = b_a.copy()

    base_sc = float(2.0 * n * float(np.max(C))) if C.size else float("inf")
    end_sc = float(2.0 * n * float(np.max(b_d))) if b_d.size else float("inf")

    if not np.isfinite(base_sc):
        base_sc = float("inf")
    if not np.isfinite(end_sc):
        end_sc = float("inf")

    if end_sc < base_sc:
        best_t = 1.0
        best_seq = d.copy()
        best_b = b_d.copy()
        best_sc = end_sc
    else:
        best_t = 0.0
        best_seq = a.copy()
        best_b = C.copy()
        best_sc = base_sc

    T = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, 65)
    sc = eval_mix_scores(A, B, C, T, n)
    order = np.argsort(sc)[:3]
    gridw = 1.0 / 64.0

    for idx in order:
        center = float(T[idx])
        width = 2.0 * gridw

        for _ in range(2):
            lo = max(0.0, center - width)
            hi = min(1.0, center + width)
            if hi - lo < 1e-7:
                break
            T2 = np.linspace(lo, hi, 25)
            sc2 = eval_mix_scores(A, B, C, T2, n)
            j = int(np.argmin(sc2))
            center = float(T2[j])
            width *= 0.4

        new = (1.0 - center) * a + center * d
        new = normalize_to_sum(new, 1.0)
        scf, bnew = score_and_conv(new, n)

        if np.isfinite(scf) and scf < best_sc:
            best_sc = scf
            best_t = center
            best_seq = new
            best_b = bnew

    return best_t, best_seq, best_b, best_sc


def make_two_block(n, frac, ratio):
    """Two constant blocks, normalized to sum 1."""
    n = int(n)
    if n < 2:
        return normalize_to_sum(np.ones(1, dtype=np.float64), 1.0)

    m = int(round(frac * float(n)))
    m = max(1, min(n - 1, m))

    a = np.empty(n, dtype=np.float64)
    a[:m] = float(ratio)
    a[m:] = 1.0
    return normalize_to_sum(a, 1.0)


def make_beta_shape(n, p, q, rng, noise=0.08):
    """
    Shape proportional to t^p (1-t)^q.
    Includes some multiplicative smooth noise if rng is provided.
    """
    n = int(n)
    if n < 2:
        return normalize_to_sum(np.ones(1, dtype=np.float64), 1.0)

    t = (np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64) + 0.5) / float(n)
    y = np.power(t, float(p)) * np.power(1.0 - t, float(q))

    if noise and rng is not None:
        y = y * np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, float(noise), n))

    y = np.maximum(y, 1e-12)
    return normalize_to_sum(y, 1.0)


def make_sinusoid(n, rng):
    """Random low-frequency shaped positive sequence."""
    n = int(n)
    if n < 2:
        return normalize_to_sum(np.ones(1, dtype=np.float64), 1.0)

    t = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n, dtype=np.float64)

    f1 = int(rng.integers(1, 5))
    ph1 = float(rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0 * np.pi))
    amp1 = float(rng.uniform(0.20, 0.80))

    f2 = int(rng.integers(1, 6))
    ph2 = float(rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0 * np.pi))
    amp2 = float(rng.uniform(0.10, 0.50))

    y = 1.0 + amp1 * np.cos(2.0 * np.pi * f1 * t + ph1)
    y += amp2 * np.sin(2.0 * np.pi * f2 * t + ph2)

    y = np.maximum(y, 0.02)
    y *= np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, 0.04, n))
    return normalize_to_sum(y, 1.0)


def make_block_shape(n, rng, num_blocks=None):
    """Random piecewise-constant positive sequence."""
    n = int(n)
    if n < 4:
        return normalize_to_sum(np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64), 1.0)

    if num_blocks is None:
        num_blocks = int(rng.integers(4, 15))
    num_blocks = int(max(2, min(num_blocks, n // 2)))

    edges = np.round(np.linspace(0.0, n, num_blocks + 1)).astype(int)
    edges[0] = 0
    edges[-1] = n

    lens = np.diff(edges)
    if np.any(lens <= 0):
        edges = np.unique(np.linspace(0.0, n, num_blocks + 1).astype(int))
        if edges[0] != 0:
            edges = np.concatenate((np.array([0], dtype=int), edges))
        if edges[-1] != n:
            edges = np.concatenate((edges, np.array([n], dtype=int)))
        if len(edges) < 2:
            return normalize_to_sum(np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64), 1.0)
        lens = np.diff(edges)

    vals = np.exp(rng.uniform(-1.0, 1.0, int(lens.size)))
    if rng.random() < 0.25:
        vals[int(rng.integers(0, vals.size))] = 0.0

    a = np.repeat(vals, lens)
    if a.size != n:
        tmp = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
        m = min(n, a.size)
        tmp[:m] = a[:m]
        a = tmp

    return normalize_to_sum(a, 1.0)


def random_shape(n, rng):
    """Random nonnegative starting shape."""
    r = float(rng.random())
    if r < 0.34:
        p = float(rng.integers(0, 5)) / 2.0
        q = float(rng.integers(0, 4)) / 2.0
        return make_beta_shape(n, p, q, rng, noise=0.08)
    if r < 0.62:
        return make_sinusoid(n, rng)
    return make_block_shape(n, rng)


def smooth_perturb(a, rng, strength):
    """Multiplicative low-freq and local perturbations."""
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).copy()
    n = a.size

    if n > 2:
        t = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n, dtype=np.float64)
        z = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
        for m in range(1, 5):
            amp = float(strength) / (1.0 + 0.5 * float(m))
            ph = float(rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0 * np.pi))
            z += amp * np.cos(2.0 * np.pi * m * t + ph)
        a *= np.exp(z)

    for _ in range(2):
        width = n // 50
        if width < 1:
            width = 1
        if width > n:
            width = n
        start = int(rng.integers(0, max(1, n - width + 1)))
        stop = min(n, start + width)
        if stop > start:
            seg = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, stop - start, dtype=np.float64)
            bump = np.sin(np.pi * seg) ** 2
            if rng.random() < 0.5:
                a[start:stop] *= np.exp(-float(strength) * bump)
            else:
                a[start:stop] *= np.exp(float(strength) * bump)

    a = np.maximum(a, 1e-18)
    return normalize_to_sum(a, 1.0)


def upsample_sequence(seq, n):
    """Interpolate a sequence to length n and normalize to sum 1."""
    seq = np.asarray(seq, dtype=np.float64)
    m = seq.size
    n = int(n)

    if m == 0:
        return normalize_to_sum(np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64), 1.0)
    if m == n:
        return normalize_to_sum(seq, 1.0)
    if m == 1:
        return normalize_to_sum(np.full(n, float(seq[0]), dtype=np.float64), 1.0)

    xold = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, m, dtype=np.float64)
    xnew = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n, dtype=np.float64)
    y = np.interp(xnew, xold, seq)
    y = np.maximum(y, 0.0)
    return normalize_to_sum(y, 1.0)


def get_parent_sequence():
    """Read optional global height_sequence_1."""
    try:
        p = globals().get("height_sequence_1", None)
    except Exception:
        p = None

    if p is None:
        return None

    try:
        arr = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64)
    except Exception:
        return None

    if arr.ndim != 1:
        arr = arr.ravel()
    if arr.size == 0:
        return None

    arr = np.where(np.isfinite(arr), arr, 0.0)
    arr = np.clip(arr, 0.0, 1000.0)
    if float(arr.sum()) <= 0.0:
        return None

    return arr


def add_start(starts, seq, n):
    """Evaluate one start and append if finite."""
    try:
        seq = normalize_to_sum(np.asarray(seq, dtype=np.float64), 1.0)
        sc, _ = score_and_conv(seq, n)
        if np.isfinite(sc):
            starts.append((float(sc), seq))
    except Exception:
        pass


def knots_to_seq(x, n, K):
    """Interpolate knot vector x of length K to sequence length n."""
    x = np.asarray(x, dtype=np.float64)
    n = int(n)
    K = int(K)

    if x.size == 0:
        return normalize_to_sum(np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64), 1.0)
    if K == 1:
        val = float(x[0]) if np.isfinite(x[0]) and x[0] > 0 else 1.0
        return normalize_to_sum(np.full(n, val, dtype=np.float64), 1.0)

    try:
        t_old = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, K, dtype=np.float64)
        t_new = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n, dtype=np.float64)
        y = np.interp(t_new, t_old, x)
    except Exception:
        y = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)

    y = np.where(np.isfinite(y), y, 0.0)
    y = np.maximum(y, 1e-12)
    return normalize_to_sum(y, 1.0)


def de_objective(x, n, K):
    """DE objective for a knot-parameterized sequence."""
    try:
        seq = knots_to_seq(np.asarray(x, dtype=np.float64), int(n), int(K))
        sc, _ = score_and_conv(seq, int(n))
        if not np.isfinite(sc):
            return 1e6
        return float(sc)
    except Exception:
        return 1e6


def get_de_starts(n, budget, seed):
    """Return a few differential-evolution starts."""
    out = []
    if differential_evolution is None:
        return out
    if budget is None or budget < 250:
        return out
    if n > 768:
        return out

    n = int(n)
    try:
        if n <= 512:
            K = 14
            maxiter = 70
            popsize = 12
            runs = 2 if float(budget) > 500 else 1
        else:
            K = 22
            maxiter = 25
            popsize = 12
            runs = 1

        bounds = [(0.0, 4.0)] * K
        for r in range(int(runs)):
            res = differential_evolution(
                de_objective,
                bounds,
                args=(n, K),
                maxiter=int(maxiter),
                popsize=int(popsize),
                tol=1e-6,
                init="latinhypercube",
                seed=int(seed + 101 * r),
                polish=False,
                workers=1
            )
            if res is not None and res.x is not None:
                seq = knots_to_seq(res.x, n, K)
                sc, _ = score_and_conv(seq, n)
                if np.isfinite(sc):
                    out.append((float(sc), seq))
    except Exception:
        pass

    return out


def generate_starts(n, parents, rng, use_de=False, de_budget=0):
    """Create a diverse start pool for a given length."""
    n = int(n)
    starts = []

    for p in parents:
        if p is not None and np.asarray(p).size > 0:
            add_start(starts, upsample_sequence(p, n), n)

    add_start(starts, np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64), n)

    pvals = (0.0, 0.5, 1.0, 2.0, 4.0)
    qvals = (0.0, 0.5, 1.0, 2.0)
    for p in pvals:
        for q in qvals:
            add_start(starts, make_beta_shape(n, p, q, rng, noise=0.02), n)

    for frac in (0.10, 0.25, 0.40, 0.50, 0.60, 0.75, 0.90):
        for ratio in (0.20, 0.50, 1.50, 3.00, 6.00):
            add_start(starts, make_two_block(n, frac, ratio), n)

    for _ in range(8):
        add_start(starts, random_shape(n, rng), n)

    if use_de:
        try:
            seed = int(rng.integers(0, 1000000))
        except Exception:
            seed = 12345
        de_starts = get_de_starts(n, de_budget, seed)
        for sc, seq in de_starts:
            add_start(starts, seq, n)

    if not starts:
        add_start(starts, np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64), n)

    starts.sort(key=_pair_score)
    return starts


def build_convolution_matrix(a):
    """
    Dense matrix A with A[k,i] = a_j if i+j=k else 0.
    Rows k=0..2n-2, columns i=0..n-1.
    """
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    n = a.size
    L = 2 * n - 1
    A = np.zeros((L, n), dtype=np.float64)

    for k in range(n):
        A[k, :k + 1] = a[k::-1]

    for k in range(n, L):
        start = k - (n - 1)
        length = L - k
        A[k, start:start + length] = a[n - 1 : n - 1 - length : -1]

    return A


def block_average(a, m):
    """Averaged blocks of a sequence; returns 1D array of length m' <= m."""
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    n = a.size
    m = int(m)

    if m <= 1:
        return np.array([float(a.mean())], dtype=np.float64)
    if m >= n:
        return a.copy()

    edges = np.linspace(0, n, m + 1).astype(int)
    edges = np.unique(edges)
    if edges[0] != 0:
        edges = np.concatenate((np.array([0], dtype=int), edges))
    if edges[-1] != n:
        edges = np.concatenate((edges, np.array([n], dtype=int)))

    if len(edges) < 2:
        return np.array([float(a.mean())], dtype=np.float64)

    mt = len(edges) - 1
    sums = np.zeros(mt, dtype=np.float64)
    counts = np.diff(edges)
    for i in range(mt):
        sums[i] = float(a[edges[i]:edges[i + 1]].sum())

    return sums / np.maximum(counts, 1)


def expand_block_direction(h, n, m):
    """Expand a coarse length-m direction to length n and normalize."""
    h = np.asarray(h, dtype=np.float64)
    n = int(n)
    m = int(m)

    if h.size == 0:
        return normalize_to_sum(np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64), 1.0)

    k = n // m
    if k < 1:
        k = 1

    d = np.repeat(h, k)
    if d.size < n:
        pad_val = float(d[-1]) if d.size > 0 else 1.0
        d = np.concatenate((d, np.full(n - d.size, pad_val, dtype=np.float64)))
    elif d.size > n:
        d = d[:n]

    d = np.where(np.isfinite(d), d, 0.0)
    d = np.clip(d, 0.0, 1000.0)
    return normalize_to_sum(d, 1.0)


def lp_direction(a, seed, max_var, time_left=30.0):
    """
    Frank-Wolfe-like LP direction:
       maximize w . h subject to A(a) h <= 1, 0 <= h <= max_var.
    The returned direction is normalized to sum 1.
    """
    if linprog is None:
        return None

    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    n = a.size
    if n < 2:
        return None

    # Make every column strictly positive to avoid unbounded LP variables.
    floor = 1e-5
    a_use = np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
    a_use = np.clip(a_use, 0.0, 1000.0) + floor

    try:
        A = build_convolution_matrix(a_use)
    except Exception:
        return None

    one = np.ones(2 * n - 1, dtype=np.float64)

    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    w = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
    w += 0.20 * rng.standard_normal(n)
    w = np.clip(w, 0.05, 10.0)
    c = -w

    bnds = [(0.0, float(max_var))] * n
    res = None

    try:
        tl = int(max(1, min(60, max(1.0, float(time_left)))))
        res = linprog(
            c,
            A_ub=A,
            b_ub=one,
            bounds=bnds,
            method="highs",
            options={"time_limit": tl}
        )
    except Exception:
        res = None

    if res is None:
        try:
            res = linprog(c, A_ub=A, b_ub=one, bounds=bnds, method="highs")
        except Exception:
            res = None

    if res is None:
        try:
            res = linprog(c, A_ub=A, b_ub=one, bounds=bnds)
        except Exception:
            return None

    x = getattr(res, "x", None)
    if x is None:
        return None

    h = np.asarray(x, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    h = np.where(np.isfinite(h), h, 0.0)
    h = np.clip(h, 0.0, float(max_var))

    s = float(h.sum())
    if s <= 1e-15:
        return None

    return h / s


def coarse_lp_direction(a, seed, max_var, time_left=30.0):
    """
    LP direction on a coarse block average, then expanded.
    Useful for large lengths where dense full LP is too expensive.
    """
    if linprog is None:
        return None

    n = len(a)
    if n < 8:
        return None

    if n > 3000:
        m = 128
    else:
        m = 256

    if m >= n:
        m = max(16, n // 8)
    if m < 2:
        return None

    try:
        ac = block_average(a, m)
        mt = ac.size
        if mt < 2:
            return None

        h = lp_direction(
            ac,
            seed,
            max(10.0, 20.0 * float(mt)),
            time_left
        )
    except Exception:
        return None

    if h is None:
        return None

    return expand_block_direction(h, n, mt)


def local_search(a, budget_s, seed, allow_lp=True):
    """
    Hybrid local search:
      - active-weight multiplicative gradient steps,
      - exact line searches,
      - full or coarse LP directions,
      - smooth restarts on stagnation.
    """
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    if a.size == 0:
        a = normalize_to_sum(np.ones(1024, dtype=np.float64), 1.0)

    n = int(a.size)
    a = normalize_to_sum(a, 1.0)

    t0 = time.monotonic()
    deadline = t0 + max(0.0, float(budget_s))

    score, b = score_and_conv(a, n)
    best = a.copy()
    best_score = score
    best_b = b.copy()

    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    it = 0
    stagn = 0
    eta = 0.18
    last_lp = t0 - 1000.0

    full_lp_max = 1024
    coarse_lp_max = 4096
    allow_lp = bool(allow_lp) and (linprog is not None)

    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
        rem = deadline - time.monotonic()
        if rem < 3.0:
            break

        it += 1

        if not np.all(np.isfinite(a)) or not np.all(np.isfinite(b)) or float(a.sum()) <= 1e-300:
            a = best.copy()
            b = best_b.copy()
            score = best_score

        improved = False

        M = float(np.max(b)) if b.size else 0.0
        lams = make_lambda_list(b, M, it, rng, max_count=3)

        for lam in lams:
            r = compute_r(a, lam)
            if r.size != n:
                continue

            rmax = float(np.max(r)) if r.size else 0.0
            if not np.isfinite(rmax) or rmax <= 1e-300:
                continue

            rn = r / rmax
            meanrn = float(np.mean(rn))

            delta = -eta * (rn - meanrn)
            if rng.random() < 0.05:
                delta = delta + rng.normal(0.0, 0.02, n)
            delta = np.clip(delta, -4.0, 4.0)

            cand = a * np.exp(delta)
            cand = np.maximum(cand, 1e-18)
            cand = normalize_to_sum(cand, 1.0)

            cs, cb = score_and_conv(cand, n)

            new_seq = cand
            new_b = cb
            ns = cs

            if np.isfinite(cs) and cs < score * 1.05 + 1e-12:
                _, new_seq, new_b, ns = line_search(a, b, cand, cb, n)

            if np.isfinite(ns) and ns < score - 1e-14:
                a = new_seq
                b = new_b
                score = ns
                improved = True
                if ns < best_score - 1e-14:
                    best = a.copy()
                    best_score = ns
                    best_b = b.copy()
                eta = min(0.85, eta * 1.06)
                break
            else:
                eta = max(0.03, eta * 0.85)

        if not improved:
            use_full = allow_lp and (n <= full_lp_max)
            use_coarse = allow_lp and (n > full_lp_max) and (n <= coarse_lp_max)

            if use_full or use_coarse:
                lp_wait = 6.0 if use_full else 22.0
                if time.monotonic() - last_lp > lp_wait:
                    last_lp = time.monotonic()
                    max_dirs = 2 if n <= 600 else 1

                    for d_idx in range(max_dirs):
                        rem2 = deadline - time.monotonic() - 35.0
                        if rem2 < 20.0:
                            break

                        tl = min(35.0, max(5.0, 0.7 * rem2))
                        if use_full:
                            d = lp_direction(
                                a,
                                int(seed + 100000 + 7919 * it + d_idx),
                                max(100.0, 20.0 * float(n)),
                                tl
                            )
                        else:
                            d = coarse_lp_direction(
                                a,
                                int(seed + 200000 + 7919 * it + d_idx),
                                max(10.0, 1000.0),
                                tl
                            )

                        if d is None:
                            continue

                        try:
                            sc_d, bd = score_and_conv(d, n)
                            if not np.isfinite(sc_d):
                                continue
                            cr = fft_convolve(a, d)
                            _, new_seq, new_b, ns = line_search(a, b, d, bd, n, cr=cr)
                        except Exception:
                            continue

                        if np.isfinite(ns) and ns < score - 1e-14:
                            a = new_seq
                            b = new_b
                            score = ns
                            improved = True
                            if ns < best_score - 1e-14:
                                best = a.copy()
                                best_score = ns
                                best_b = b.copy()
                            break

        if improved:
            stagn = 0
        else:
            stagn += 1

            if stagn > 15:
                strength = 0.10 + 0.50 * float(rng.random())
                pert = smooth_perturb(best, rng, strength)
                ps, pb = score_and_conv(pert, n)

                if np.isfinite(ps) and ps < best_score:
                    best = pert
                    best_score = ps
                    best_b = pb

                mix = 0.25 + 0.50 * float(rng.random())
                a = (1.0 - mix) * best + mix * pert
                a = normalize_to_sum(a, 1.0)
                score, b = score_and_conv(a, n)

                if score > best_score * 1.05 + 1e-12:
                    a = best.copy()
                    b = best_b.copy()
                    score = best_score

                eta = 0.10 + 0.30 * float(rng.random())
                stagn = 0

            if stagn > 60:
                shape = random_shape(n, rng)
                mix = 0.30 + 0.20 * float(rng.random())
                a = (1.0 - mix) * best + mix * shape
                a = normalize_to_sum(a, 1.0)
                score, b = score_and_conv(a, n)

                if score > best_score * 1.10 + 1e-12:
                    a = best.copy()
                    b = best_b.copy()
                    score = best_score

                stagn = 0

    return best, best_score


def make_lambda_list(b, M, it, rng, max_count=3):
    """
    Return a small rotating set of coefficient weight vectors lam over
    convolution coefficients. Weights are used to form subgradients.
    """
    b = np.asarray(b, dtype=np.float64)
    L = b.size
    if L == 0:
        return []

    lams = []

    lam = np.zeros(L, dtype=np.float64)
    lam[int(np.argmax(b))] = 1.0
    lams.append(lam)

    for frac in (0.03, 0.15, 0.50):
        K = int(np.ceil(frac * float(L)))
        K = max(1, min(L, K))
        if K >= L:
            lam = np.full(L, 1.0 / float(L), dtype=np.float64)
        else:
            lam = np.zeros(L, dtype=np.float64)
            idx = np.argpartition(b, L - K)[-K:]
            lam[idx] = 1.0 / float(K)
        lams.append(lam)

    meanb = float(np.mean(b))
    spread = M - meanb
    if not np.isfinite(spread) or spread <= 1e-300:
        spread = M if M > 0 else 1.0

    for Tfrac in (0.10, 0.30):
        T = max(1e-300, float(Tfrac) * float(spread))
        floor = M - 5.0 * float(spread)
        if floor < 0.0:
            floor = 0.0

        exponent = (b - floor) / T
        exponent = np.clip(exponent, -50.0, 50.0)
        w = np.exp(exponent)
        s = float(w.sum())
        if s <= 1e-300:
            w = np.full(L, 1.0, dtype=np.float64)
            w[int(np.argmax(b))] = 5.0
            s = float(w.sum())

        if s > 1e-300:
            lams.append(w / s)
        else:
            lams.append(np.full(L, 1.0 / float(L), dtype=np.float64))

    lams.append(np.full(L, 1.0 / float(L), dtype=np.float64))

    if len(lams) <= max_count:
        return lams

    selected = [lams[0]]
    pool = lams[1:]
    if len(pool) <= max_count - 1:
        selected.extend(pool)
    else:
        rot = int(it) % len(pool)
        for ii in range(max_count - 1):
            selected.append(pool[(rot + ii) % len(pool)])

    return selected


def add_candidate(pool, seq, sc):
    """Keep a small pool of finite candidates."""
    try:
        sc = float(sc)
    except Exception:
        return

    if seq is None:
        return
    if not np.isfinite(sc):
        return

    try:
        arr = np.asarray(seq, dtype=np.float64).copy()
    except Exception:
        return

    pool.append((sc, arr))
    pool.sort(key=_pair_score)
    if len(pool) > 12:
        pool.pop()


def run_level(n, budget_s, parents, seed, allow_lp=True):
    """Generate starts at length n and refine the best few."""
    n = int(n)
    t0 = time.monotonic()
    deadline = t0 + max(0.0, float(budget_s))

    if budget_s < 30:
        if parents is not None and len(parents) > 0:
            seq = upsample_sequence(parents[0], n)
        else:
            seq = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
        seq = normalize_to_sum(seq, 1.0)
        sc, _ = score_and_conv(seq, n)
        return seq, sc

    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)

    use_de = (
        differential_evolution is not None and
        n <= 768 and
        float(budget_s) > 250
    )
    de_budget = min(150.0, 0.15 * float(budget_s)) if use_de else 0

    try:
        starts = generate_starts(n, parents, rng, use_de=use_de, de_budget=de_budget)
    except Exception:
        starts = [(float("inf"), normalize_to_sum(np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64), 1.0))]

    if not starts:
        starts = [(2.0, normalize_to_sum(np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64), 1.0))]

    k = 1
    if float(budget_s) > 300:
        k = 2
    if float(budget_s) > 600 and n <= 1024:
        k = 3
    k = max(1, min(k, len(starts)))

    if k == 1:
        fracs = (1.0,)
    elif k == 2:
        fracs = (0.62, 0.34)
    else:
        fracs = (0.55, 0.30, 0.15)

    best_seq = None
    best_score = float("inf")

    for i in range(k):
        remain = deadline - time.monotonic()
        if i == k - 1:
            time_this = max(0.0, remain - 4.0)
        else:
            if remain < 30.0:
                break
            time_this = max(15.0, remain * fracs[i])

        if time_this <= 8.0:
            break

        try:
            start_seq, start_sc = starts[i]
            seq, sc = local_search(
                start_seq,
                time_this,
                int(seed * 10 + 17 + i),
                allow_lp=allow_lp
            )

            if not np.isfinite(sc):
                sc, _ = score_and_conv(start_seq, n)

            if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score:
                best_seq = seq
                best_score = sc
                print(f"[run_level n={n}] start {i}: {sc:.7f}")
        except Exception:
            continue

    if best_seq is None:
        best_seq, best_score = starts[0][1], starts[0][0]
        if not np.isfinite(best_score):
            best_seq = normalize_to_sum(np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64), 1.0)
            best_score, _ = score_and_conv(best_seq, n)

    return best_seq, best_score


def sanitize_for_return(seq):
    """Return a safe Python list of finite nonnegative floats with sum 1."""
    a = np.asarray(seq, dtype=np.float64)
    if a.ndim != 1:
        a = a.ravel()
    if a.size == 0:
        return [1.0]

    a = np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
    a = np.clip(a, 0.0, 1000.0)

    s = float(a.sum())
    if s <= 0.01:
        return [1.0]

    a = a / s
    a = np.clip(a, 0.0, 1000.0)

    if float(a.sum()) <= 0.01:
        return [1.0]

    return [float(x) for x in a]


def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=2400, **kwargs):
    """
    Entry point.
    Coarse-to-fine search over sequence lengths, starting from the previous
    best if available, and returns the best exact-validated list.
    """
    t0 = time.monotonic()
    budget = max(30.0, float(budget_s))

    parent = get_parent_sequence()
    if parent is None or parent.size < 16:
        base_n = 512
        base_seq = np.ones(base_n, dtype=np.float64)
    else:
        pn = int(parent.size)
        if 128 <= pn <= 2048:
            base_n = pn
            base_seq = normalize_to_sum(parent, 1.0)
        elif pn > 2048:
            base_n = 1024
            base_seq = upsample_sequence(parent, base_n)
        else:
            base_n = 512
            base_seq = upsample_sequence(parent, base_n)

    sc0, _ = score_and_conv(base_seq, base_n)
    candidates = []
    add_candidate(candidates, base_seq, sc0)
    print(f"[init] base_n={base_n} fast_score={sc0:.7f}")

    levels = [base_n]
    cur = base_n
    while len(levels) < 3:
        nxt = min(4096, 2 * cur)
        if nxt <= cur:
            break
        levels.append(nxt)
        cur = nxt

    search_budget = max(60.0, budget - 150.0)

    if search_budget < 350 or len(levels) == 1:
        chosen = levels[:1]
        weights = (1.0,)
    elif search_budget < 900 or len(levels) == 2:
        chosen = levels[:2]
        weights = (0.50, 0.50)
    else:
        chosen = levels[:3]
        weights = (0.34, 0.32, 0.34)

    budgets = [search_budget * w for w in weights]
    total = sum(budgets)
    if total > 0:
        budgets = [max(20.0, b / total * search_budget) for b in budgets]
        if len(budgets) > 1:
            budgets[-1] = max(10.0, search_budget - sum(budgets[:-1]))

    parents = [base_seq.copy()]

    for idx, (lv, bud) in enumerate(zip(chosen, budgets)):
        if time.monotonic() > t0 + budget - 150.0:
            break
        try:
            seq, sc = run_level(
                int(lv),
                max(10.0, float(bud)),
                parents,
                int(seed + 1000 * idx + lv),
                allow_lp=True
            )
        except Exception:
            if parents:
                seq = upsample_sequence(parents[0], int(lv))
            else:
                seq = np.ones(int(lv), dtype=np.float64)
            seq = normalize_to_sum(seq, 1.0)
            sc, _ = score_and_conv(seq, int(lv))

        add_candidate(candidates, seq, sc)
        parents.append(seq)
        if len(parents) > 3:
            parents = parents[-3:]

        print(f"[level {lv}] best_fast_score={float(sc):.7f}")

    if not candidates:
        fallback = normalize_to_sum(np.ones(1024, dtype=np.float64), 1.0)
        candidates.append((2.0, fallback))

    candidates.sort(key=_pair_score)
    exact_best_seq = candidates[0][1]
    exact_best_score = float(candidates[0][0])

    # Exact validation of the top fast candidates.
    max_exact = 6
    try:
        if np.asarray(candidates[0][1]).size > 2048:
            max_exact = 3
    except Exception:
        pass

    exact_called = 0
    for sc_fast, seq in candidates:
        if exact_called >= max_exact:
            break
        if time.monotonic() > t0 + budget - 25.0:
            break

        try:
            exact = float(evaluate_sequence([float(x) for x in np.asarray(seq)]))
        except Exception:
            exact = float("inf")

        exact_called += 1
        if np.isfinite(exact) and exact < exact_best_score:
            exact_best_score = exact
            exact_best_seq = seq

    if exact_best_seq is None or (not np.isfinite(exact_best_score)):
        exact_best_seq = candidates[0][1]

    final_list = sanitize_for_return(exact_best_seq)
    print(f"[final] length={len(final_list)} exact_or_fast_bound={float(exact_best_score):.7f}")
    return final_list
```
